It runs on\n376\tevery mapping run, incremental (`--paths`) and full alike. `--files` narrows it\n377\tto the documents an Update run actually refreshed; `--paths` needs no narrowing\n378\tbecause all seven are regenerated, just scoped in content.\n379\t\n380\t**Why this is a shell step and not an instruction to the mapper.** The stamp is\n381\tthe only machine-readable freshness marker: the `verify codebase-drift` gate\n382\treads it to decide what to diff HEAD against. The human-readable markers the\n383\tmapper writes (`**Analysis Date:**`, `<!-- refreshed: ... -->`) are restamped\n384\tunconditionally on an Update run, so a mapper that decides its work is already\n385\tdone and rewrites only the dates still looks fresh to a human. Leaving the\n386\tmachine-readable stamp to the same agent reproduces exactly the failure the\n387\tstamp exists to detect. A shell step cannot be skipped by a confident agent.\n388\t\n389\tThe command is non-blocking: it emits `skipped` with a `reason` outside a git\n390\trepo or when no documents exist. Report `stamped` and `commit` in the summary\n391\tif any entry in `failed` is non-empty; otherwise continue silently.\n392\t\n393\tRun in this position, before `commit_codebase_map`, the stamp lands on\n394\tdocuments the mapper just wrote, so its markdown whitespace normalization is\n395\tfolded into the same commit. Running `stamp-codebase-map` by hand against an\n396\talready-committed map reflows that map's whitespace as a side effect.\n397\t\n398\tContinue to scan_for_secrets.\n399\t</step>\n400\t\n401\t<step name=\"scan_for_secrets\">\n402\t**CRITICAL SECURITY CHECK:** Scan output files for accidentally leaked secrets before committing.\n403\t\n404\tRun secret pattern detection:\n405\t\n406\t```bash\n407\t# Check for common API key patterns in generated docs\n408\tgrep -E '(sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|sk_live_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|sk_test_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|ghp_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|gho_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|glpat-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+|AKIA[A-Z0-9]{16}|xox[baprs]-[a-zA-Z0-9-]+|[REDACTED:gsd-scan]|eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.)' .planning/codebase/*.md 2>/dev/null && SECRETS_FOUND=true || SECRETS_FOUND=false\n```\n\n**If SECRETS_FOUND=true:**\n\n```\n⚠️  SECURITY ALERT: Potential secrets detected in codebase documents!\n\nFound patterns that look like API keys or tokens in:\n[show grep output]\n\nThis would expose credentials if committed.\n\n**Action required:**\n1. Review the flagged content above\n2. If these are real secrets, they must be removed before committing\n3. Consider adding sensitive files to Claude Code \"Deny\" permissions\n\nPausing before commit. Reply \"safe to proceed\" if the flagged content is not actually sensitive, or edit the files first.\n```\n\nWait for user confirmation before continuing to commit_codebase_map.\n\n**If SECRETS_FOUND=false:**\n\nContinue to commit_codebase_map.\n</step>\n\n<step name=\"commit_codebase_map\">\nCommit the codebase map:\n\n```bash\ngsd_run query commit \"docs: map existing codebase\" --files .planning/codebase/*.md\n```\n\nContinue to offer_next.\n</step>\n\n<step name=\"offer_next\">\nPresent completion summary and next steps.\n\n**Get line counts:**\n```bash\nwc -l .planning/codebase/*.md\n```\n\n**Output format:**\n\n```\nCodebase mapping complete.\n\nCreated .planning/codebase/:\n- STACK.md ([N] lines) - {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NdpxETD6dTtpPaZNeWwZux","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Architecture\n2\t\n3\tStatus: Accepted\n4\t\n5\tThis document describes how the portfolio site is structured: layers, import rules, folder layout, data flow, the content model, the design-token pipeline and the animation layer. It is written so the scaffold can be built directly from it. Decisions with a longer rationale live in the [ADR log](adr/README.md).\n6\t\n7\t## Overview\n8\t\n9\tThe site is a static, zero-backend, single-page React app served from GitHub Pages. There is no router, no global store and no data fetching. The architecture comes down to four seams:\n10\t\n11\t1. Typed content: all copy lives in TypeScript modules checked against one schema.\n12\t2. CSS-variable design tokens: five role colors per theme drive every surface.\n13\t3. An internal animation layer: the only place the two animation libraries are imported.\n14\t4. A build and deploy pipeline: Vite build, GitHub Actions, GitHub Pages.\n15\t\n16\tEverything else is conventional React composition with props flowing down.\n17\t\n18\t## Layers\n19\t\n20\tDependencies point down only. A layer may import from layers below it, never above.\n21\t\n22\t```text\n23\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n24\t| app/        main.tsx, App.tsx, Providers, Page.tsx                    |\n25\t|             Page.tsx is the ONLY file that imports content data       |\n26\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n27\t| sections/   Hero, About, Experience, Projects, Skills, Education,     |\n28\t|             Contact                      (props in, JSX out)          |\n29\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n30\t| layout/     Container, Section, SiteHeader, PillNav, Footer,          |\n31\t|             SkipLink, Wordmark                                        |\n32\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n33\t| components/ ui/ (CtaLink, Toast, ThemeToggle, icons)                  |\n34\t|             motion-primitives/ (vendored source, treated as owned)    |\n35\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n36\t| anim/       the internal animation layer: the only place that         |\n37\t|             imports motion/react and @react-spring/web               |\n38\t+-------------------------------+--------------------------------------+\n39\t| hooks/  lib/                  | styles/ (tokens.css, base.css,       |\n40\t| (useActiveSection, useTheme,  |  index.css = tailwind + theme map)   |\n41\t|  cn, a11y)                    |                                      |\n42\t+-------------------------------+--------------------------------------+\n43\t| content/ (typed TS data)      assets/ (images, fonts via Fontsource) |\n44\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n45\tBuild and deploy: vite.config.ts, lint boundary rules, GitHub Actions\n46\t```\n47\t\n48\t## Import rules\n49\t\n50\tOnly src/anim/ and src/components/motion-primitives/ may import motion/react or @react-spring/web.\n51\t\n52\tOnly src/app/Page.tsx imports content data.\n53\t\n54\t| Importer | May import | May not import |\n55\t|----------|-----------|----------------|\n56\t| sections/* | layout, components/ui, the anim barrel (src/anim/index.ts), content types (not data) | motion/react, @react-spring/*, components/motion-primitives/* directly, content data |\n57\t| layout/*, components/ui/* | the anim barrel, lib, hooks | sections, content data |\n58\t| components/motion-primitives/* | motion/react, lib/cn | everything else (vendor code, kept out of lint autofix churn) |\n59\t| anim/* | motion/react, @react-spring/web, components/motion-primitives/*, the token mirror in styles | sections, layout |\n60\t| app/Page.tsx | content data, sections | any animation library or anim internals |\n61\t\n62\tThese rules are enforced by oxlint's `no-restricted-imports` rule with per-folder overrides in `.oxlintrc.json`, covering dynamic `import()` and re-exports as well, so a violation fails CI rather than relying on review. Unit tests under `tests/unit` may import the libraries for assertions. A gesture library is not adopted up front. It would be added to the anim row only if a react-spring drag cannot be built well otherwise, after a React 19 compatibility check.\n63\t\n64\t## Folder structure\n65\t\n66\t```text\n67\trepo root\n68\t|-- public/                  URL-stable files only: favicon set, og image,\n69\t|                            resume.pdf (a phone-number-free export), robots.txt\n70\t|-- index.html               meta and social tags, inline theme-init script,\n71\t|                            noscript fallback content\n72\t|-- 404.html                 second HTML entry: the not-found page that\n73\t|                            GitHub Pages serves for unknown paths\n74\t|-- vite.config.ts           base from env, Tailwind plugin, imagetools, \"@\" alias\n75\t|-- .oxlintrc.json           import-boundary rules (see Import rules)\n76\t|-- .github/workflows/       ci.yml, deploy.yml\n77\t`-- src/\n78\t    |-- main.tsx\n79\t    |-- app/                 App.tsx, Page.tsx, Providers.tsx\n80\t    |-- sections/            one folder per section (Hero, About, Experience,\n81\t    |                        Projects, Skills, Education, Contact)\n82\t    |-- layout/              Container, Section, SiteHeader, PillNav,\n83\t    |                        Footer, SkipLink, Wordmark\n84\t    |-- components/\n85\t    |   |-- ui/              CtaLink, Toast, ThemeToggle, icons/ (inline SVG\n86\t    |   |                    brand marks and UI glyphs)\n87\t    |   `-- motion-primitives/   vendored, patched locally, logged in an ADR\n88\t    |-- anim/\n89\t    |   |-- index.ts         public barrel: the only animation import path\n90\t    |   |-- MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx\n91\t    |   |-- useMotionPreference.ts\n92\t    |   |-- tokens.ts        springs, durations, eases in both libraries' formats\n93\t    |   |-- MotionFeatures.tsx   LazyMotion provider with the async feature chunk\n94\t    |   |-- declarative/     motion-owned wrappers (ToastPresence,\n95\t    |   |                    NavIndicator, PillMorph, TextReveal)\n96\t    |   `-- physics/         react-spring-owned wrappers (ThemeThumb and other\n97\t    |                        pointer-driven, velocity-carrying helpers)\n98\t    |-- content/\n99\t    |   |-- schema.ts        every type\n100\t    |   |-- profile.ts, experience.ts, projects.ts, skills.ts,\n101\t    |   |   education.ts, contacts.ts     one module per section\n102\t    |   |-- images.ts        image registry\n103\t    |   `-- index.ts         assembles siteContent with satisfies\n104\t    |-- hooks/               useActiveSection, useTheme\n105\t    |-- lib/                 cn.ts, date formatting, a11y helpers\n106\t    |-- styles/\n107\t    |   |-- index.css        tailwind import, dark variant, theme mapping\n108\t    |   |-- tokens.css       role tokens, derived tokens, light and dark blocks\n109\t    |   `-- base.css         resets, focus ring, reduced-motion guard, fonts\n110\t    `-- assets/images/       hashed by Vite\n111\t```\n112\t\n113\tThe animation folder is named anim/ rather than motion/ so it is never confused with the motion package or its motion/react import path.\n114\t\n115\t## Data flow\n116\t\n117\tBuild time:\n118\t\n119\t```text\n120\tcontent/*.ts --(typed, satisfies)--> app/Page --props--> sections --props--> layout, ui\n121\tassets/*     --(imagetools, hashed imports)--> image fields in content\n122\t```\n123\t\n124\tRuntime, all client-side with no network use beyond same-origin assets:\n125\t\n126\t- Theme: an inline script in the document head reads localStorage, falls back to prefers-color-scheme, and sets the data-theme attribute on the html element before first paint. The theme provider in React only changes the attribute and storage on a user toggle.\n127\t- Motion preference: the OS reduced-motion setting (plus an optional site override) feeds one hook, useMotionPreference, which configures both animation libraries and the wrapper components. See Reduced motion.\n128\t- Scroll-spy: an IntersectionObserver over the section ids drives useActiveSection, which highlights the current section in the navigation.\n129\t- Pointer and touch input drive react-spring values written straight to the DOM, with no React re-render per frame.\n130\t- Project detail: a click or tap sets local state in the Projects section, which opens an overlay with focus handling.\n131\t\n132\tThere is no global state library. Three small contexts exist: theme, motion preference and, optionally, the active section. Everything else is props.\n133\t\n134\t## Content model\n135\t\n136\tAll types live in src/content/schema.ts:\n137\t\n138\t- Profile: name, tagline, bio paragraphs, optional quote, photo (ImageAsset), contacts.\n139\t- ContactLink: kind (email, github, linkedin or instagram), label, href, whether it is external.\n140\t- ExperienceEntry: id, role, org, optional org URL, optional logo, dates (DateRange), location, bullets, optional highlights and stack.\n141\t- Project: id, title, context kind (personal, employer, course or hackathon) with a label, dates, stack, bullets, relatedExperienceId, featured, optional links and image.\n142\t- EducationEntry: id, degree, school, location, dates, gpa, optional track.\n143\t- SkillGroup: id, label, items as a string array.\n144\t- DateRange: ISO start and end (or present) plus a display label in one normalized format.\n145\t- ImageAsset: src, alt, width, height, optional srcset.\n146\t\n147\tRules:\n148\t\n149\t- Content modules use satisfies against the schema so a missing field fails typecheck.\n150\t- Ordering lives in the data (array order or a priority field), never in components.\n151\t- Copy is taken verbatim from the reviewed content inventory; any edit is recorded there with before and after, not made silently in code.\n152\t- The featured flag decides what appears first on mobile, so \"key info in seconds\" is a data decision.\n153\t- Content tests check that ids are unique, related ids resolve, every image has alt text, every external link is https, and dates parse in descending order.\n154\t- A phone number is not a field anywhere in the model, and must never appear in site copy, metadata or the served resume.\n155\t\n156\t## Design tokens\n157\t\n158\t- Exactly five role tokens per theme: text, background, primary, secondary, accent. Light and dark are authored separately, not inverted.\n159\t- Derived tokens (surface, border, muted text, link, focus ring and so on) are computed from the five roles with color-mix(in oklch, ...). A palette swap therefore edits one block and every derived value follows.\n160\t- The theme is selected by the data-theme attribute on the html element.\n161\t- Tailwind v4 is configured CSS-first: a custom dark variant is bound to [data-theme=dark] with @custom-variant, and an @theme inline block maps utilities to the CSS variables. There is no tailwind.config.js.\n162\t- No raw hex values in components. Components use semantic utilities only; a CI check greps for stray hex.\n163\t- A contrast test recomputes every text, UI and focus pair in both themes against WCAG 2.2 AA (4.5:1 for body text, 3:1 for large text and UI) and fails the build on any miss.\n164\t- Spring and duration values that JavaScript needs are mirrored in CSS variables, with src/anim/tokens.ts as the canonical source for springs.\n165\t\n166\t## Animation layer\n167\t\n168\tsrc/anim/index.ts is the only animation import path for the rest of the app. It exposes a handful of semantic components and hooks, not library primitives.\n169\t\n170\t| Interaction class | Owner |\n171\t|-------------------|-------|\n172\t| Mount and unmount, presence, exit animations | motion |\n173\t| Layout and shared-element transitions | motion |\n174\t| Scroll-linked values and in-view reveals | motion |\n175\t| Text and number effects, stagger, ready-made motion-primitives prefabs | motion |\n176\t| Velocity-carrying physics: press-squish, rail hover lift with neighbor shift, theme toggle thumb | react-spring |\n177\t| Color and hover transitions, native scroll-snap rail swipe | CSS |\n178\t| Desktop wheel smoothing only | Lenis |\n179\t\n180\tThe rule: one library animates any given element. Never both libraries on one element or one property, and no Tailwind transition utilities on an element a library animates. If a vendored primitive duplicates a physics wrapper, one implementation is chosen per interaction and the other stays unused.\n181\t\n182\tSpring and duration tokens are defined once in src/anim/tokens.ts, exported in both libraries' formats (motion's stiffness and damping, react-spring's tension and friction), so the two feel like one system. Fine-pointer effects such as tilt, magnetic pull and spotlight are gated by (hover: hover) and (pointer: fine); touch gets a tap or press equivalent, or nothing. Every wrapper reads the motion preference, renders an instant equivalent when reduced, forwards className, and never hides content in its initial CSS state.\n183\t\n184\t## Reduced motion\n185\t\n186\tOne preference source, useMotionPreference, combines the OS prefers-reduced-motion setting with an optional site override and feeds three consumers:\n187\t\n188\t- motion: the provider renders MotionConfig with its reducedMotion setting. This disables transform and layout animation but not opacity or color, so wrappers handle parallax, scroll-linked and autoplay effects explicitly.\n189\t- react-spring: the provider calls Globals.assign({ skipAnimation }) so every spring jumps to its target; the effect cleans up on change.\n190\t- CSS: a @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) guard in base.css shortens transitions and animations and keeps scroll behavior native.\n191\t\n192\tContent is visible by default and is never hidden until JavaScript runs. Hidden initial states are applied client-side inside wrappers, and a noscript rule guarantees visibility. Pressed, focused and selected states must read without motion (color, outline, shape). Lenis is off under reduced motion. The provider listens for changes to the media query so a live OS toggle takes effect without a reload.\n193\t\n194\t## Assets\n195\t\n196\t- Images live in src/assets/images/ and are imported as modules (hashed, long-cached). vite-imagetools produces AVIF and WebP variants and srcset with intrinsic width and height to prevent layout shift. Below-the-fold images lazy-load; the hero image is high priority.\n197\t- Fonts are self-hosted through Fontsource (variable, latin subset, font-display swap), at most two families. No third-party font CDN.\n198\t- Brand icons (GitHub, LinkedIn, Instagram, email) and UI glyphs (close, chevrons, download) are inline SVG components under src/components/ui/icons/. No icon package is installed. See ADR-0005 and ADR-0010 in the ADR log.\n199\t- The resume PDF served from public/ is the owner's phone-number-free export of the resume. The phone-number gate reads its text layer, link annotations and metadata, and rejects a stand-in file.\n200\t- Only images that are actually used migrate from the previous site, with EXIF metadata stripped. Template leftovers are left behind.\n201\t\n202\t## Build and deploy\n203\t\n204\tGitHub Actions builds the site and publishes it to GitHub Pages. The Vite base comes from the VITE_BASE environment variable: the project URL path (for example /aa-phan-portfolio-v2/) while staging, and / after the cutover to the user-site URL. Assets are module-imported so no absolute asset paths exist; anything under public/ that JavaScript references goes through import.meta.env.BASE_URL. CI also builds with the project-path base so a broken sub-path is caught before launch. The build has a second HTML entry, 404.html, a static not-found page that GitHub Pages serves for unknown paths. Steps, settings, cutover and rollback are in the deploy runbook (deploy-runbook.md).\n205\t\n206\t## Quality gates\n207\t\n208\t| Gate | Command | Fails the build when |\n209\t|------|---------|----------------------|\n210\t| Typecheck | `tsc -b` | A content field is missing or a reference is wrong |\n211\t| Lint with import boundaries | oxlint, warnings denied | A section imports an animation library or content data |\n212\t| Format check | Prettier with Tailwind class sorting | A file is not formatted |\n213\t| Unit tests | Vitest: shell parity, theme logic, motion preference, gate negatives | Any test fails |\n214\t| Path-case check | `scripts/check-path-case.mjs`, run locally and on Linux CI | An import differs in case from the file on disk |\n215\t| Raw color check | `scripts/check-colors.mjs` | Source outside the token file and vendored primitives holds a raw hex, rgb, hsl, oklch or oklab value, or a Tailwind default palette utility |\n216\t| Token contrast test | `scripts/check-contrast.mjs` | Any text, UI or focus pair is below AA in any theme |\n217\t| Build | `vite build` | The Vite build errors |\n218\t| Initial JS budget | `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs` | The module script and modulepreloads exceed 150 kB gzipped |\n219\t| Phone-number check | `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs` over the built site | A served file holds a phone-shaped string or a tel: link, or a PDF's text layer, link annotations or metadata does (read with pdfjs-dist), or the PDF cannot be read, or resume.pdf is not a real resume (the owner's name and at least 1500 non-whitespace characters of text) |\n220\t| Image check | `scripts/check-images.mjs` over dist, public and src | A shipped raster is not AVIF or WebP, carries Exif or XMP, or is unreferenced, or a source image is not imported |\n221\t| Smoke tests | Playwright in CI | The hero is missing with JavaScript off, the theme flashes, or content is hidden or a transform animation runs with reduced motion emulated |\n222\t| axe | Later gate | Serious or critical violations in light or dark |\n223\t| Mobile Lighthouse | Later gate | Targets set in the scaffold milestone are missed |\n224\t\n225\t`npm run verify` chains the first eleven rows in that order, and both CI workflows call it.\n226\t\n227\t## Stack\n228\t\n229\tVerified 2026-10-08; re-verify at scaffold.\n230\t\n231\t| Technology | Version | Purpose |\n232\t|------------|---------|---------|\n233\t| Vite | ^8.3.4 | Build and dev server |\n234\t| React and React DOM | ^19.3.0 | UI |\n235\t| TypeScript | ~6.0.3 | Types |\n236\t| @vitejs/plugin-react | ^6.1.2 | React transform |\n237\t| Tailwind CSS and @tailwindcss/vite | ^4.3.3 | Styling, first-party Vite plugin |\n238\t| motion | ~14.0.0 | Declarative animation, imported from motion/react |\n239\t| @react-spring/web | ~10.1.2 | Spring physics |\n240\t| clsx | ^2.1.1 | Class composition |\n241\t| tailwind-merge | ^3.7.0 | Class conflict resolution |\n242\t| lenis | 1.3.x | Desktop wheel smoothing |\n243\t| vite-imagetools | ^12.0.1 | Image resize and format variants |\n244\t| pdfjs-dist | 6.3.289 (exact, dev only) | Phone-number gate: reads the served PDF's text, links and metadata |\n245\t| axe-core | 4.13.0 (dev only) | Accessibility pass of the visual QA run |\n246\t\n247\tUI glyphs are inline SVG (ADR-0010), refreshed: 2026-11-19 -->\n# Architecture\n\n**Analysis Date:** 2026-11-19\n\n## System Overview\n\n```text\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                 Browser (static, client-only)               │\n│   index.html -> src/main.tsx -> App -> Providers -> Page    │\n└──────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────┘\n                           │ props only, no network calls\n                           ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ Sections   `src/sections/*`  Hero, Experience, Projects,    │\n│            About, Skills, Education, Contact                │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│ Layout     `src/layout/*`    SiteHeader, PillNav, Footer    │\n│ UI         `src/components/ui/*`  CtaLink, Toast, ThemeToggle│\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│ Animation  `src/anim/*`      the only motion/react and       │\n│                              @react-spring/web importer     │\n├──────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────┤\n│ Hooks / lib                  │ Styles                       │\n│ `src/hooks/*`, `src/lib/*`   │ `src/styles/*` (Tailwind v4) │\n├──────────────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────┤\n│ Data (build time)  `src/content/*`   Assets `src/assets/*`  │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n## Component Responsibilities\n\n| Component | Responsibility | File |\n|-----------|----------------|------|\n| Bootstrap | Mounts the React root in `StrictMode`, loads the Archivo font CSS and global styles | `src/main.tsx` |\n| App shell | Composes providers and the page | `src/app/App.tsx` |\n| Providers | Wraps the tree in motion preference, lazy motion features and toast context | `src/app/Providers.tsx` |\n| Page | The only file that imports content data; wires props to sections and layout; runs the scroll-spy | `src/app/Page.tsx` |\n| Content model | Typed data per section, assembled into `siteContent` | `src/content/index.ts`, `src/content/schema.ts` |\n| Scroll-spy | IntersectionObserver over section ids; drives nav highlight | `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` |\n| Theme store | Reads and writes `html[data-theme]` and localStorage; `useSyncExternalStore` | `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`, `src/lib/theme.ts` |\n| Motion preference | Combines OS reduced-motion with a site override; configures both animation libraries | `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`, `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx` |\n| Lazy motion features | Loads the motion `domMax` feature bundle after mount; never settles under reduced motion | `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`, `src/anim/features.ts` |\n| Animation barrel | The single public animation import path | `src/anim/index.ts` |\n| Header and nav | Desktop header and mobile pill nav, both fed by `activeId` and `lockTo` | `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx`, `src/layout/PillNav.tsx`, `src/layout/nav-items.ts` |\n| Projects | Project sleeve list and the detail dialog | `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`, `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx` |\n| Toast | Toast context, hook and presence wrapper | `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/components/ui/useToast.ts`, `src/anim/declarative/ToastPresence.tsx` |\n| Copy email | Clipboard write with execCommand fallback, then mail fallback | `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`, `src/lib/copy.ts` |\n| Vendored text effect | Vendored motion-primitives component; exempt from import rules | `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx` |\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Static, single-page React application with build-time typed content, a single page composition component, and a one-way props hierarchy.\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- No backend, router or global store. Three small contexts exist: theme (via `useSyncExternalStore`), motion preference and toast.\n- Content is typed TypeScript data validated by `satisfies` against `src/content/schema.ts`, so missing fields fail `tsc -b`.\n- Only `src/anim/` imports animation libraries; `src/app/Page.tsx` is the only importer of content data. Both rules are enforced by oxlint in `.oxlintrc.json`.\n- Styling is CSS-variable driven: five role colors per theme in `src/styles/tokens.css`, mapped to Tailwind utilities in `src/styles/index.css`.\n- Output is a static bundle with two HTML entries (`index.html`, `404.html`).\n\n## Layers\n\n**App (composition):**\n- Purpose: Assemble providers and the page\n- Location: `src/app/`\n- Contains: `App.tsx`, `Providers.tsx`, `Page.tsx`\n- Depends on: `src/anim` barrel, `src/components/ui/Toast`, `src/content` (Page only), `src/sections`, `src/layout`, `src/hooks`\n- Used by: `src/main.tsx`\n\n**Sections:**\n- Purpose: Render each portfolio section from props\n- Location: `src/sections/<Name>/`\n- Contains: one folder per section (`Hero`, `Experience`, `Projects`, `About`, `Skills`, `Education`, `Contact`)\n- Depends on: `src/layout`, `src/components/ui`, the `@/anim` barrel, content types from `src/content/schema.ts`\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n**Layout and UI:**\n- Purpose: Shared page chrome and small reusable widgets\n- Location: `src/layout/` and `src/components/ui/`\n- Contains: `Container`, `Section`, `SiteHeader`, `PillNav`, `Footer`, `SkipLink`, `Wordmark`, `CtaLink`, `ThemeToggle`, `Toast`, icon components in `src/components/ui/icons/`\n- Depends on: `src/anim` barrel, `src/lib`, `src/hooks`\n- Used by: sections and `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n**Animation:**\n- Purpose: The only place that imports `motion/react` and `@react-spring/web`\n- Location: `src/anim/`\n- Contains: providers, `declarative/` wrappers (motion-owned), `physics/` wrappers (react-spring-owned), `tokens.ts`\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `@react-spring/web`, `src/components/motion-primitives/` (per the barrel), the token mirror in styles\n- Must not import: `src/sections`, `src/layout`, `src/content` (enforced by an override in `.oxlintrc.json`)\n- Used by: layout, UI, sections via `src/anim/index.ts`\n\n**Vendored primitives:**\n- Purpose: Third-party-derived component source kept in-repo\n- Location: `src/components/motion-primitives/`\n- Contains: `text-effect.tsx` (imports `motion/react` directly; lint rules are off for this folder)\n- Used by: the animation barrel\n\n**Hooks and lib:**\n- Purpose: Framework-agnostic and reusable logic\n- Location: `src/hooks/` (`useActiveSection.ts`, `useTheme.ts`) and `src/lib/` (`theme.ts`, `copy.ts`, `utils.ts`, `copy.ts`)\n- Depends on: React and browser APIs only\n- Used by: layout, UI, app\n\n**Content and assets:**\n- Purpose: Typed site data and build-time image imports\n- Location: `src/content/` and `src/assets/images/`\n- Contains: `schema.ts`, `profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `skills.ts`, `education.ts`, `contacts.ts`, `images.ts`, `index.ts`\n- Depends on: `vite-imagetools` query imports (`src/content/images.ts`)\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx` only\n\n**Styles:**\n- Purpose: Design tokens, theme blocks, base rules\n- Location: `src/styles/`\n- Contains: `index.css` (Tailwind import, dark variant, theme mapping), `tokens.css` (role tokens, light and dark blocks), `base.css`, `interactions.css`, `not-found.css`\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (page load)\n\n1. `index.html` loads the module entry and runs the theme init script before first paint (per `docs/architecture.md`)\n2. `src/main.tsx` imports the font and global CSS, then renders `<App />` into `#root`\n3. `src/app/App.tsx` wraps `<Page />` in `Providers`\n4. `src/app/Providers.tsx` mounts `MotionPreferenceProvider`, then `MotionFeatures` (LazyMotion), then `ToastProvider`\n5. `src/app/Page.tsx` reads `siteContent` from `src/content/index.ts`, derives the email and the non-email contacts, and passes props to each section\n6. `useActiveSection(SECTION_IDS)` in `src/app/Page.tsx` drives `activeId` for `SiteHeader` and `PillNav`; `lockTo` is passed back as `onNavigate`\n\n### Theme Toggle\n\n1. `ThemeToggle` (`src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx`) calls `toggle` from `useTheme`\n2. `useTheme` (`src/hooks/useTheme.ts`) calls `applyTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts`, which sets the `data-theme` attribute and localStorage key `theme`\n3. Subscribers via `useSyncExternalStore(subscribeTheme, readTheme)` re-render; `subscribeTheme` also follows the OS scheme and other tabs\n\n### Motion Preference\n\n1. `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts` reads `prefers-reduced-motion` and the site override\n2. `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx` sets `MotionConfig` (motion) and `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation })` (react-spring)\n3. `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx` loads `src/anim/features.ts` after first idle time; under reduced motion the loader never settles\n\n### Project Detail\n\n1. Click or tap in `src/sections/Projects/Sleeve.tsx` sets selected state in `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`\n2. `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx` renders the detail overlay with focus handling\n\n### Copy Email\n\n1. `useCopyEmail` (`src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`) calls `copyText` from `src/lib/copy.ts`\n2. On failure the caller opens the `mailto:` link\n\n**State Management:**\n- Local component state for UI toggles and the project dialog\n- Three contexts only: theme (external store), motion preference, toast\n- No global store; everything else flows through props from `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**Content types:**\n- Purpose: Shape of all site data\n- Examples: `src/content/schema.ts` (`Profile`, `ContactLink`, `ExperienceEntry`, `Project`, `EducationEntry`, `SkillGroup`, `DateRange`, `ImageAsset`)\n- Pattern: `satisfies` against the schema in each content module; ordering lives in data\n\n**Animation barrel:**\n- Purpose: Semantic animation API for the rest of the app\n- Examples: `src/anim/index.ts`, `src/anim/declarative/NavIndicator.tsx`, `src/anim/declarative/PillMorph.tsx`, `src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx`, `src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`\n- Pattern: one library per element; every wrapper honours the motion preference and forwards `className`\n\n**Tokens:**\n- Purpose: Single source for spring and duration values\n- Examples: `src/anim/tokens.ts` (canonical springs, exported in motion and react-spring formats); `src/styles/tokens.css` (CSS mirror)\n- Pattern: Five role colors per theme; derived colors computed with `color-mix(in oklch, ...)`\n\n**Theme store:**\n- Purpose: One source of truth for the theme\n- Examples: `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`\n- Pattern: DOM attribute is the source; React subscribes via `useSyncExternalStore`\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**Browser entry (SPA):**\n- Location: `index.html` -> `src/main.tsx`\n- Triggers: Page load of the base path\n- Responsibilities: Mount the React tree, load styles and the Archivo font\n\n**Not-found entry:**\n- Location: `404.html`\n- Triggers: GitHub Pages serving unknown paths\n- Responsibilities: Static not-found page; styled by `src/styles/not-found.css`; an e2e spec `tests/e2e/not-found.spec.ts` covers it\n\n**Build and tooling entries:**\n- `vite.config.ts` - Vite build and dev server\n- `scripts/check-*.mjs` - quality gates run by `npm run verify`\n- `.github/workflows/ci.yml` and `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` - CI and deploy\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No workers. Pointer-driven react-spring values write to the DOM directly without React re-renders per frame.\n- **Global state:** `document.documentElement` `data-theme` attribute and the `theme` localStorage key (`src/lib/theme.ts`); module-level values in `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts`\n- **Circular imports:** None detected in the files read for this map.\n- **Import boundaries:** Enforced by `.oxlintrc.json`: animation libraries only under `src/anim` and `src/components/motion-primitives`; content data only from `src/app/Page.tsx`; `src/anim` may not import sections, layout or content.\n- **Runtime network:** None beyond same-origin assets; resume and images are same-origin.\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\nThese are the patterns the lint rules and docs forbid. The current tree follows them; keep it that way when adding code.\n\n### Importing animation libraries from a section\n\n**What happens:** A component imports `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web` directly.\n**Why it's wrong:** It bypasses the motion preference wiring and the one-library-per-element rule in `docs/architecture.md`; oxlint fails the build.\n**Do this instead:** Import from `@/anim` (see `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx` and `src/layout/PillNav.tsx` for the pattern).\n\n### Importing content data outside Page\n\n**What happens:** A section or layout file imports `@/content` data directly.\n**Why it's wrong:** Breaks the one-way data flow; oxlint fails the build.\n**Do this instead:** Add the prop in `src/app/Page.tsx` and pass it down. Types may still come from `src/content/schema.ts`.\n\n### Hidden content until JavaScript runs\n\n**What happens:** An animation wrapper starts content at `opacity: 0` or `transform` in its initial CSS state.\n**Why it's wrong:** Content must stay visible without JavaScript and under reduced motion; the smoke tests check this.\n**Do this instead:** Apply hidden initial states client-side inside the wrapper, and rely on the `noscript` rule in `index.html` for the fallback.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Degrade silently. Missing browser features turn the feature off rather than throwing.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- `IntersectionObserver` missing: `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` returns early and `activeId` stays null\n- `matchMedia` missing or blocked: `src/lib/theme.ts` ignores OS scheme changes; `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts` checks `hasMatchMedia()`\n- Clipboard blocked: `src/lib/copy.ts` falls back to `execCommand`, then the caller opens mail\n- Failed motion feature chunk: `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx` returns a never-settling promise, so `m` components stay static\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** None. No logging library and no `console` usage in the runtime path was relied on.\n**Validation:** Compile-time, via `satisfies` against `src/content/schema.ts`; test-time, via `tests/unit/content.test.ts` and `tests/unit/content-inventory.test.ts`; build-time, via `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs` and `scripts/check-images.mjs`.\n**Authentication:** None.\n\n---\n\n*Architecture analysis: 2026-11-19*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6351,"cache_read_input_tokens":64810,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":6351,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01Gh7udCjipE6kECBKCyJkDc","agent_id":"a8ec551090f22b350","session_id":"9007afe3-53c4-4825-bde0-5acfef19849a","uuid":"4f159ff7-c3bf-4013-8660-811723f79e7a","timestamp":"2026-11-20T02:39:22.367Z","request_id":"req_011ChDQSsqQUU6BNfcBv9wb3","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Map codebase tech and architecture"}

helper), `copy.ts`\n\n**`src/styles/`:**\n- Purpose: Tailwind entry and design tokens\n- Contains: `index.css` (Tailwind import, custom dark variant, theme mapping), `tokens.css` (role and derived tokens, light and dark blocks), `base.css` (resets, focus, reduced-motion guard), `interactions.css`, `not-found.css`\n\n**`src/assets/images/`:**\n- Purpose: Images imported as modules so Vite hashes by `npm run verify` and `check:*`\n- Contains: `check-colors.mjs`, `check-contrast.mjs`, `check-images.mjs`, `check-js-budget.mjs`, `check-no-phone.mjs`, `check-path-case.mjs`, `check-qa-shots.mjs`, File Locations\n\n**Entry Points:**\n- `index.html`: main HTML entry, loads `src/main.tsx`\n- `src/main.tsx`: React root and global CSS\n- `404.html`: not-found HTML entry\n\n**Configuration:**\n- `vite.config.ts`: base path, plugins, alias, inputs\n- `package.json`: scripts (`verify` chain) and dependencies\n- `tsconfig.app.json`: app compiler options and `@/*` path\n- `.oxlintrc.json`: lint and import-boundary rules\n- `prettier.config.mjs`: formatting\n- `vitest.config.ts`, `playwright.config.ts`: test runners\n\n**Core Logic:**\n- `src/app/Page.tsx`: page composition and scroll-spy wiring\n- `src/anim/index.ts`: animation API\n- `src/lib/theme.ts`: theme storage and DOM attribute\n- `src/content/schema.ts`: content types\n\n**Testing:**\n- `tests/unit/`: Vitest\n- `tests/e2e/`: Playwright\n\n## Naming Conventions\n\n**Files:**\n- React components: PascalCase `.tsx`, e.g. `src/layout/PillNav.tsx`, `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`\n- Hooks: `use` prefix in camelCase `.ts`/`.tsx`, e.g. `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`, `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`\n- Non-component modules: camelCase, e.g. `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`, `src/lib/copy.ts`, `src/anim/tokens.ts`\n- Mixed: kebab-case for some module names, e.g. `src/layout/nav-items.ts`, `src/sections/Projects/project-types.ts`, `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts` (camelCase despite the name)\n- Tests: `*.test.ts` / `*.test.tsx` in `tests/unit/`; `*.spec.ts` in `tests/e2e/`\n- Scripts: `check-*.mjs` in `scripts/`\n\n**Directories:**\n- Section folders: PascalCase, e.g. `src/sections/Projects/`\n- Other folders: lowercase, e.g. `src/anim/declarative/`, `src/components/ui/icons/`\n\n**Imports:**\n- Use the `@/` alias for anything under `src/`, e.g. `import { useTheme } from '@/hooks/useTheme'`\n- Import animation through `@/anim` only (never `@/anim/declarative/...` from outside `src/anim`)\n- `src/components/motion-primitives/` is imported from within `src/anim/` only\n\n## Where to Add New Code\n\n**New Section:**\n- Implementation: `src/sections/<Name>/<Name>.tsx`, props-in and JSX-out\n- Data: a new module in `src/content/` with a type in `src/content/schema.ts`, exported from `src/content/index.ts`\n- Wiring: add the section to `src/app/Page.tsx` and its id to `src/layout/nav-items.ts`\n- Tests: `tests/unit/sections.test.tsx` or a new file in `tests/unit/`; an e2e spec in `tests/e2e/` if it changes navigation\n\n**New Layout or UI component:**\n- Implementation: `src/layout/<Name>.tsx` for page chrome, `src/components/ui/<Name>.tsx` for reusable widgets\n- Icons: inline SVG under `src/components/ui/icons/` (no icon package; see ADR-0010)\n\n**New Animation:**\n- Wrapper: `src/anim/declarative/` (motion-owned) or `src/anim/physics/` (react-spring-owned)\n- Export through `src/anim/index.ts` only\n- Spring and duration values go in `src/anim/tokens.ts`\n\n**New Hook:**\n- Shared: `src/hooks/<useName>.ts`\n- Component-local: next to the component\n\n**Utilities:**\n- Shared helpers: `src/lib/`\n\n**Styles and tokens:**\n- Colors and theme roles: `src/styles/tokens.css` (role tokens per theme); do not put raw hex in components (`scripts/check-colors.mjs`)\n- Tailwind mapping: `src/styles/index.css`\n\n**Static files and images:**\n- URL-stable files: `public/` (reference through `import.meta.env.BASE_URL`)\n- Images: `src/assets/images/` and register in `src/content/images.ts`\n\n## Special Directories\n\n**`dist/`:**\n- Purpose: Vite build output\n- Generated: Yes\n- Committed: No Overview\n\n```text\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                 Browser (static, client-only)               │\n│   index.html -> src/main.tsx -> App -> Providers -> Page    │\n└──────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────┘\n                           │ props only, no network calls\n                           ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ Sections   `src/sections/*`  Hero, Experience, Projects,    │\n│            About, Skills, Education, Contact                │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│ Layout     `src/layout/*`    SiteHeader, PillNav, Footer    │\n│ UI         `src/components/ui/*`  CtaLink, Toast, ThemeToggle│\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│ Animation  `src/anim/*`      the only motion/react and       │\n│                              @react-spring/web importer     │\n├──────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────┤\n│ Hooks / lib                  │ Styles                       │\n│ `src/hooks/*`, `src/lib/*`   │ `src/styles/*` (Tailwind v4) │\n├──────────────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────┤\n│ Data (build time)  `src/content/*`   Assets `src/assets/*`  │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n## Component Responsibilities\n\n| Component | Responsibility | File |\n|-----------|----------------|------|\n| Bootstrap | Mounts the React root in `StrictMode`, loads the Archivo font CSS and global styles | `src/main.tsx` |\n| App shell | Composes providers and the page | `src/app/App.tsx` |\n| Providers | Wraps the tree in motion preference, lazy motion features and toast context | `src/app/Providers.tsx` |\n| Page | The only file that imports content data; wires props to sections and layout; runs the scroll-spy | `src/app/Page.tsx` |\n| Content model | Typed data per section, assembled into `siteContent` | `src/content/index.ts`, `src/content/schema.ts` |\n| Scroll-spy | IntersectionObserver over section ids; drives nav highlight | `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` |\n| Theme store | Reads and writes `html[data-theme]` and localStorage; `useSyncExternalStore` | `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`, `src/lib/theme.ts` |\n| Motion preference | Combines OS reduced-motion with a site override; configures both animation libraries | `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`, `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx` |\n| Lazy motion features | Loads the motion `domMax` feature bundle after mount; never settles under reduced motion | `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`, `src/anim/features.ts` |\n| Animation barrel | The single public animation import path | `src/anim/index.ts` |\n| Header and nav | Desktop header and mobile pill nav, both fed by `activeId` and `lockTo` | `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx`, `src/layout/PillNav.tsx`, `src/layout/nav-items.ts` |\n| Projects | Project sleeve list and the detail dialog | `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`, `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx` |\n| Toast | Toast context, hook and presence wrapper | `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/components/ui/useToast.ts`, `src/anim/declarative/ToastPresence.tsx` |\n| Copy email | Clipboard write with execCommand fallback, then mail fallback | `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`, `src/lib/copy.ts` |\n| Vendored text effect | Vendored motion-primitives component; exempt from import rules | `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx` |\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Static, single-page React application with build-time typed content, a single page composition component, and a one-way props hierarchy.\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- No backend, router or global store. Three small contexts exist: theme (via `useSyncExternalStore`), motion preference and toast.\n- Content is typed TypeScript data validated by `satisfies` against `src/content/schema.ts`, so missing fields fail `tsc -b`.\n- Only `src/anim/` imports animation libraries; `src/app/Page.tsx` is the only importer of content data. Both rules are enforced by oxlint in `.oxlintrc.json`.\n- Styling is CSS-variable driven: five role colors per theme in `src/styles/tokens.css`, mapped to Tailwind utilities in `src/styles/index.css`.\n- Output is a static bundle with two HTML entries (`index.html`, `404.html`).\n\n## Layers\n\n**App (composition):**\n- Purpose: Assemble providers and the page\n- Location: `src/app/`\n- Contains: `App.tsx`, `Providers.tsx`, `Page.tsx`\n- Depends on: `src/anim` barrel, `src/components/ui/Toast`, `src/content` (Page only), `src/sections`, `src/layout`, `src/hooks`\n- Used by: `src/main.tsx`\n\n**Sections:**\n- Purpose: Render each portfolio section from props\n- Location: `src/sections/<Name>/`\n- Contains: one folder per section (`Hero`, `Experience`, `Projects`, `About`, `Skills`, `Education`, `Contact`)\n- Depends on: `src/layout`, `src/components/ui`, the `@/anim` barrel, content types from `src/content/schema.ts`\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n**Layout and UI:**\n- Purpose: Shared page chrome and small reusable widgets\n- Location: `src/layout/` and `src/components/ui/`\n- Contains: `Container`, `Section`, `SiteHeader`, `PillNav`, `Footer`, `SkipLink`, `Wordmark`, `CtaLink`, `ThemeToggle`, `Toast`, icon components in `src/components/ui/icons/`\n- Depends on: `src/anim` barrel, `src/lib`, `src/hooks`\n- Used by: sections and `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n**Animation:**\n- Purpose: The only place that imports `motion/react` and `@react-spring/web`\n- Location: `src/anim/`\n- Contains: providers, `declarative/` wrappers (motion-owned), `physics/` wrappers (react-spring-owned), `tokens.ts`\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `@react-spring/web`, `src/components/motion-primitives/` (per the barrel), the token mirror in styles\n- Must not import: `src/sections`, `src/layout`, `src/content` (enforced by an override in `.oxlintrc.json`)\n- Used by: layout, UI, sections via `src/anim/index.ts`\n\n**Vendored primitives:**\n- Purpose: Third-party-derived component source kept in-repo\n- Location: `src/components/motion-primitives/`\n- Contains: `text-effect.tsx` (imports `motion/react` directly; lint rules are off for this folder)\n- Used by: the animation barrel\n\n**Hooks and lib:**\n- Purpose: Framework-agnostic and reusable logic\n- Location: `src/hooks/` (`useActiveSection.ts`, `useTheme.ts`) and `src/lib/` (`theme.ts`, `copy.ts`, `utils.ts`, `copy.ts`)\n- Depends on: React and browser APIs only\n- Used by: layout, UI, app\n\n**Content and assets:**\n- Purpose: Typed site data and build-time image imports\n- Location: `src/content/` and `src/assets/images/`\n- Contains: `schema.ts`, `profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `skills.ts`, `education.ts`, `contacts.ts`, `images.ts`, `index.ts`\n- Depends on: `vite-imagetools` query imports (`src/content/images.ts`)\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx` only\n\n**Styles:**\n- Purpose: Design tokens, theme blocks, base rules\n- Location: `src/styles/`\n- Contains: `index.css` (Tailwind import, dark variant, theme mapping), `tokens.css` (role tokens, light and dark blocks), `base.css`, `interactions.css`, `not-found.css`\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (page load)\n\n1. `index.html` loads the module entry and runs the theme init script before first paint (per `docs/architecture.md`)\n2. `src/main.tsx` imports the font and global CSS, then renders `<App />` into `#root`\n3. `src/app/App.tsx` wraps `<Page />` in `Providers`\n4. `src/app/Providers.tsx` mounts `MotionPreferenceProvider`, then `MotionFeatures` (LazyMotion), then `ToastProvider`\n5. `src/app/Page.tsx` reads `siteContent` from `src/content/index.ts`, derives the email and the non-email contacts, and passes props to each section\n6. `useActiveSection(SECTION_IDS)` in `src/app/Page.tsx` drives `activeId` for `SiteHeader` and `PillNav`; `lockTo` is passed back as `onNavigate`\n\n### Theme Toggle\n\n1. `ThemeToggle` (`src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx`) calls `toggle` from `useTheme`\n2. `useTheme` (`src/hooks/useTheme.ts`) calls `applyTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts`, which sets the `data-theme` attribute and localStorage key `theme`\n3. Subscribers via `useSyncExternalStore(subscribeTheme, readTheme)` re-render; `subscribeTheme` also follows the OS scheme and other tabs\n\n### Motion Preference\n\n1. `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts` reads `prefers-reduced-motion` and the site override\n2. `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx` sets `MotionConfig` (motion) and `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation })` (react-spring)\n3. `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx` loads `src/anim/features.ts` after first idle time; under reduced motion the loader never settles\n\n### Project Detail\n\n1. Click or tap in `src/sections/Projects/Sleeve.tsx` sets selected state in `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`\n2. `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx` renders the detail overlay with focus handling\n\n### Copy Email\n\n1. `useCopyEmail` (`src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`) calls `copyText` from `src/lib/copy.ts`\n2. On failure the caller opens the `mailto:` link\n\n**State Management:**\n- Local component state for UI toggles and the project dialog\n- Three contexts only: theme (external store), motion preference, toast\n- No global store; everything else flows through props from `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**Content types:**\n- Purpose: Shape of all site data\n- Examples: `src/content/schema.ts` (`Profile`, `ContactLink`, `ExperienceEntry`, `Project`, `EducationEntry`, `SkillGroup`, `DateRange`, `ImageAsset`)\n- Pattern: `satisfies` against the schema in each content module; ordering lives in data\n\n**Animation barrel:**\n- Purpose: Semantic animation API for the rest of the app\n- Examples: `src/anim/index.ts`, `src/anim/declarative/NavIndicator.tsx`, `src/anim/declarative/PillMorph.tsx`, `src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx`, `src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`\n- Pattern: one library per element; every wrapper honours the motion preference and forwards `className`\n\n**Tokens:**\n- Purpose: Single source for spring and duration values\n- Examples: `src/anim/tokens.ts` (canonical springs, exported in motion and react-spring formats); `src/styles/tokens.css` (CSS mirror)\n- Pattern: Five role colors per theme; derived colors computed with `color-mix(in oklch, ...)`\n\n**Theme store:**\n- Purpose: One source of truth for the theme\n- Examples: `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`\n- Pattern: DOM attribute is the source; React subscribes via `useSyncExternalStore`\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**Browser entry (SPA):**\n- Location: `index.html` -> `src/main.tsx`\n- Triggers: Page load of the base path\n- Responsibilities: Mount the React tree, load styles and the Archivo font\n\n**Not-found entry:**\n- Location: `404.html`\n- Triggers: GitHub Pages serving unknown paths\n- Responsibilities: Static not-found page; styled by `src/styles/not-found.css`; an e2e spec `tests/e2e/not-found.spec.ts` covers it\n\n**Build and tooling entries:**\n- `vite.config.ts` - Vite build and dev server\n- `scripts/check-*.mjs` - quality gates run by `npm run verify`\n- `.github/workflows/ci.yml` and `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` - CI and deploy\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No workers. Pointer-driven react-spring values write to the DOM directly without React re-renders per frame.\n- **Global state:** `document.documentElement` `data-theme` attribute and the `theme` localStorage key (`src/lib/theme.ts`); module-level values in `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts`\n- **Circular imports:** None detected in the files read for this map.\n- **Import boundaries:** Enforced by `.oxlintrc.json`: animation libraries only under `src/anim` and `src/components/motion-primitives`; content data only from `src/app/Page.tsx`; `src/anim` may not import sections, layout or content.\n- **Runtime network:** None beyond same-origin assets; resume and images are same-origin.\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\nThese are the patterns the lint rules and docs forbid. The current tree follows them; keep it that way when adding code.\n\n### Importing animation libraries from a section\n\n**What happens:** A component imports `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web` directly.\n**Why it's wrong:** It bypasses the motion preference wiring and the one-library-per-element rule in `docs/architecture.md`; oxlint fails the build.\n**Do this instead:** Import from `@/anim` (see `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx` and `src/layout/PillNav.tsx` for the pattern).\n\n### Importing content data outside Page\n\n**What happens:** A section or layout file imports `@/content` data directly.\n**Why it's wrong:** Breaks the one-way data flow; oxlint fails the build.\n**Do this instead:** Add the prop in `src/app/Page.tsx` and pass it down. Types may still come from `src/content/schema.ts`.\n\n### Hidden content until JavaScript runs\n\n**What happens:** An animation wrapper starts content at `opacity: 0` or `transform` in its initial CSS state.\n**Why it's wrong:** Content must stay visible without JavaScript and under reduced motion; the smoke tests check this.\n**Do this instead:** Apply hidden initial states client-side inside the wrapper, and rely on the `noscript` rule in `index.html` for the fallback.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Degrade silently. Missing browser features turn the feature off rather than throwing.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- `IntersectionObserver` missing: `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` returns early and `activeId` stays null\n- `matchMedia` missing or blocked: `src/lib/theme.ts` ignores OS scheme changes; `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts` checks `hasMatchMedia()`\n- Clipboard blocked: `src/lib/copy.ts` falls back to `execCommand`, then the caller opens mail\n- Failed motion feature chunk: `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx` returns a never-settling promise, so `m` components stay static\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** None. No logging library and no `console` usage in the runtime path was relied on.\n**Validation:** Compile-time, via `satisfies` against `src/content/schema.ts`; test-time, via `tests/unit/content.test.ts` and `tests/unit/content-inventory.test.ts`; build-time, via `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs` and `scripts/check-images.mjs`.\n**Authentication:** None.\n\n---\n\n*Architecture analysis: 2026-11-19*\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":99,"oldLines":7,"newStart":99,"newLines":7,"lines":[" "," **Hooks and lib:**"," - Purpose: Framework-agnostic and reusable logic","-- Location: `src/hooks/` (`useActiveSection.ts`, `useTheme.ts`) and `src/lib/` (`theme.ts`, `copy.ts`, `utils.ts`, `copy.ts`)","+- Location: `src/hooks/` (`useActiveSection.ts`, `useTheme.ts`) and `src/lib/` (`theme.ts`, `copy.ts`, `utils.ts`)"," - Depends on: React and browser APIs only"," - Used by: layout, UI, app"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false,"contentNotInModelContext":true},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_018fGoYgP3teaiBifScpxJM4","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"rule"}}]}

`src/anim/declarative/`, `src/components/ui/icons/`\n\n**Imports:**\n- Use the `@/` alias for anything under `src/`, e.g. `import { useTheme } from '@/hooks/useTheme'`\n- Import animation through `@/anim` only (never `@/anim/declarative/...` from outside `src/anim`)\n- `src/components/motion-primitives/` is imported from within `src/anim/` only\n\n## Where to Add New Code\n\n**New
